30:["$","li",null,{"style":{"marginBottom":"0.35em","fontSize":"1rem"},"children":[["$","strong",null,{"style":{"color":"var(--text-strong)","fontWeight":600},"children":"Automatic (algorithmic) differentiation (AD):"}]," Compute derivatives by applying the chain rule to the ",["$","strong",null,{"style":{"color":"var(--text-strong)","fontWeight":600},"children":"sequence of elementary operations"}]," that the program executes. AD evaluates the derivative at a specific numeric input, producing exact (to machine precision) derivatives at a cost proportional to evaluating the function itself. This is what PyTorch, JAX, and TensorFlow do."]}]
